问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据证据，U1700 的各引脚与网络的连接关系如下： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **支撑证据**：来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中的 pin mapping 记录（`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700`），明确列出了 U1700 所有引脚的对端网络名称。
评分正文：根据证据，U1700 的各引脚与网络的连接关系如下： - nINT → MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1 - EN1 → MCU_5V_EN - VIN_B0_1 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_1 → VCC3V3 - VANA → VCC3V3 - SW_BST → N19935658 - NRST → N19935946 - SDA(EN3) → MCU_SDA_P15.5 - SCL(EN2) → MCU_SCL_P15.4 - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - CLKIN(GPO2) → MCU_WD_INH - WDI → MCU_WDI_P13.3 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - PG1(GPO1) → N19936014 - WD_RESET → TC367_RESET_N - SW_B1_1 → N19935668 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - SW_B0_1 → N19935443 - SW_B0_2 → N19935443 - VIN_B0_2 → VCC3V3 - VIN_B1_2 → VCC3V3 - SW_B1_2 → N19935668 - PAD → GND - AGND → GND - PGND_BST → GND - PGND_B1_2 → GND - PGND_B1_1 → GND - PGND_B0_2 → GND - PGND_B0_1 → GND **支撑证据**：来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中的 pin mapping 记录（`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700`），明确列出了 U1700 所有引脚的对端网络名称。

问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：基于现有证据，网络VCC3V3上直接连接的主要芯片如下： **结论：** 网络VCC3V3直接连接的主要芯片包括：**U1802 (TPS22918)**、**U1804 (SA23002C)**，以及位号为 **U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800** 的器件（具体型号未知）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **直接连接的芯片及其型号：** * **U1802 (TPS22918)**：证据[19]显示，实例U1802的型号为TPS22918。证据[1]显示，U1802的VIN引脚连接到网络VCC3V3。 * **U1804 (SA23002C)**：证据[20]显示，实例U1804的型号为SA23002C。证据[1]显示，U1804的VIN引脚连接到网络VCC3V3。 2. **直接连接但型号未知的芯片：** * 证据[1]显示，网络VCC3V3还直接连接了以下器件的电源/输入引脚，但其具体芯片型号（如U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800）在现有证据中未明确给出。 * U1500.VIN * U1600.VCCIN * U1700.VIN_B0_1, U1700.VIN_B1_1, U1700.VANA, U1700.VIN_B0_2, U1700.VIN_B1_2 * U1800.VIN, U1800.MODE/SYNC **缺失信息：** * **芯片型号缺失**：网络VCC3V3直接连接的芯片U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800的具体型号，在提供的证据中未明确说明。 * **子网络关系**：证据[9]和[14]的文本描述中提到，VCC3V3是系统一级电源，通过LN10046产生，并作为其他电源芯片（如LP87702、TPS62872）的输入。然而，这些芯片（LN10046、LP87702、TPS62872）在电路设计证据[1]中并未直接列出其引脚连接到网络"VCC3V3"，因此无法确认其直接连接关系。
评分正文：基于现有证据，网络VCC3V3上直接连接的主要芯片如下： **结论：** 网络VCC3V3直接连接的主要芯片包括：**U1802 (TPS22918)**、**U1804 (SA23002C)**，以及位号为 **U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800** 的器件（具体型号未知）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **直接连接的芯片及其型号：** * **U1802 (TPS22918)**：证据[19]显示，实例U1802的型号为TPS22918。证据[1]显示，U1802的VIN引脚连接到网络VCC3V3。 * **U1804 (SA23002C)**：证据[20]显示，实例U1804的型号为SA23002C。证据[1]显示，U1804的VIN引脚连接到网络VCC3V3。 2. **直接连接但型号未知的芯片：** * 证据[1]显示，网络VCC3V3还直接连接了以下器件的电源/输入引脚，但其具体芯片型号（如U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800）在现有证据中未明确给出。 * U1500.VIN * U1600.VCCIN * U1700.VIN_B0_1, U1700.VIN_B1_1, U1700.VANA, U1700.VIN_B0_2, U1700.VIN_B1_2 * U1800.VIN, U1800.MODE/SYNC **缺失信息：** * **芯片型号缺失**：网络VCC3V3直接连接的芯片U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800的具体型号，在提供的证据中未明确说明。 * **子网络关系**：证据[9]和[14]的文本描述中提到，VCC3V3是系统一级电源，通过LN10046产生，并作为其他电源芯片（如LP87702、TPS62872）的输入。然而，这些芯片（LN10046、LP87702、TPS62872）在电路设计证据[1]中并未直接列出其引脚连接到网络"VCC3V3"，因此无法确认其直接连接关系。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含以下 **12 个模块电路**： 1. KL30 电源管理电路 2. 唤醒电路 3. 电源电路 4. TC377 最小系统 5. EQ6 最小系统 6. 摄像头最小系统 7. 看门狗电路 8. 温度采样电路 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源 10. ETH 电路 11. CAN/CAN FD 电路 12. 3V3 过压保护电路 **支撑证据**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的目录明确列出 “2.3 模块电路” 下的 12 个子章节（2.3.1 至 2.3.12），每个子章节对应上述一个模块电路[3]。
评分正文：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含以下 **12 个模块电路**： 1. KL30 电源管理电路 2. 唤醒电路 3. 电源电路 4. TC377 最小系统 5. EQ6 最小系统 6. 摄像头最小系统 7. 看门狗电路 8. 温度采样电路 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源 10. ETH 电路 11. CAN/CAN FD 电路 12. 3V3 过压保护电路 **支撑证据**：文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 的目录明确列出 “2.3 模块电路” 下的 12 个子章节（2.3.1 至 2.3.12），每个子章节对应上述一个模块电路[3]。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：根据已有证据，给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**。 ### 主 PMIC 型号：LP87702 **证据**：文档明确指出“选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案”，并详细描述了该芯片的功能和连接方式（[10] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536）。 ### 主 PMIC 输出电压 LP87702 输出以下三路电压： - **VCC1V8**：用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电。 - **VCC1V1**：用于 EQ6、DDR 供电。 - **VCC50**：用于 CAN 收发器供电。 **证据**：文档明确说明“LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50”（[10] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536）。 ### 软件配置方式 软件通过以下方式配置 LP87702： 1. **使能控制**：使用 MCU 的 **P15.3** 引脚使能 LP87702 的 5V 输出（VCC50）。 2. **I2C 通信**：通过 MCU 的 **SCL (P15.4)** 和 **SDA (P15.5)** 引脚进行 I2C 通信，用于设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压并控制其使能。 3. **复位控制**：使用 MCU 的 **P12.1** 引脚复位该芯片。 4. **运行中寄存器配置**：LP87702 运行期间，MCU 需要通过 I2C 配置特定寄存器。文档指出：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”（[12] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: d69d04d302326d46）。但该寄存器配置表格的具体内容在提供的证据中未完整呈现。 **证据**：上述使能、I2C通信及复位方式在[10] (evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536) 和 [16] (evidence_id: a6f318d1053a4c7e) 中均有明确描述。关于运行中需通过I2C配置寄存器的总述来源于[12] (evidence_id: d69d04d302326d46)。 ### 缺失信息 - **软件配置的具体寄存器设置**：虽然证据提到需要配置寄存器（Table 5-2），但该表格的具体内容（如寄存器地址、位域定义、初始化值等）在现有证据中**未完整提供**。
评分正文：根据已有证据，给板子供电的主 PMIC 是 **LP87702**。 ### 主 PMIC 型号：LP87702 **证据**：文档明确指出“选择 LP87702 PMIC 方案”，并详细描述了该芯片的功能和连接方式（[10] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536）。 ### 主 PMIC 输出电压 LP87702 输出以下三路电压： - **VCC1V8**：用于 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 供电。 - **VCC1V1**：用于 EQ6、DDR 供电。 - **VCC50**：用于 CAN 收发器供电。 **证据**：文档明确说明“LP87702 chip is used to convert VCC3V3 into VCC1V8, VCC1V1 and VCC50”（[10] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536）。 ### 软件配置方式 软件通过以下方式配置 LP87702： 1. **使能控制**：使用 MCU 的 **P15.3** 引脚使能 LP87702 的 5V 输出（VCC50）。 2. **I2C 通信**：通过 MCU 的 **SCL (P15.4)** 和 **SDA (P15.5)** 引脚进行 I2C 通信，用于设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压并控制其使能。 3. **复位控制**：使用 MCU 的 **P12.1** 引脚复位该芯片。 4. **运行中寄存器配置**：LP87702 运行期间，MCU 需要通过 I2C 配置特定寄存器。文档指出：“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”（[12] Source: 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx, evidence_id: d69d04d302326d46）。但该寄存器配置表格的具体内容在提供的证据中未完整呈现。 **证据**：上述使能、I2C通信及复位方式在[10] (evidence_id: 5e69a562758e3536) 和 [16] (evidence_id: a6f318d1053a4c7e) 中均有明确描述。关于运行中需通过I2C配置寄存器的总述来源于[12] (evidence_id: d69d04d302326d46)。 ### 缺失信息 - **软件配置的具体寄存器设置**：虽然证据提到需要配置寄存器（Table 5-2），但该表格的具体内容（如寄存器地址、位域定义、初始化值等）在现有证据中**未完整提供**。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. When VCC3V3 <VGs threshold (Q1701), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_SW has no electricity. 2.When 4.03V>VCC3V3>VGS threshold (Q1701), NMOS opens, PMOS opens, and VCC3V3_sw and VCC3V3 pass through. 3.When VCC3V3>4.03V, the cathode pin output logic is low (up to 1V), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_Sw has no power, which plays an overvoltage protection role on the load of the later stage. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | 数量统计：4; ID：86; SM Name：Processor mechanism for SPF; SM TSR：The ECU shall integrate the processing platform safety mechanism for SPF._x000D_ When the processor SPF is confirmed, the ECU shall:_x000D_ 1. stop outputing ADAS request and;_x000D_ 2. disable the CAN communication and;_x000D_ 3. reset processing platform._x000D_ FHTI < 300 ms._x000D_ When the processor SPF is no longer detected after the processor reset, the ECU shall clear the processor SPF. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_006; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：In initialization process, the SW shall configure SMU reset type to system reset. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_018; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：The SW shall enable the lockstep. ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_020; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应该避免PLFASH中出现全0或者全1。如果可能出现全0情况，软件应该使用0x36填充，不能写全1填充。 ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_021; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：软件应保证受CPU0 ENDINIT保护的寄存器被写操作时，正确解开CPU ENDINIT保护，写完寄存器后，再次锁上CPU ENDINIT保护。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护前，软件应屏蔽中断，防止写寄存器时间超时。 解开CPU ENDINIT保护后，写寄存器的时间小于1us ——SYS SM ID：SWRS_TC3XX_016; SM Name：COMMON; SM TSR：After SMU reset, the SW shall read the SMU fault status register and store SMU fault DTC and Snapshot in NVM. 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：0.75V电源由两颗独立的芯片产生，分别是**TPS62872**（产生VCC0V75）和**SA23002**（产生VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR），两者的输入均为VCC3V3。文档中对应的配置要求包括使能控制、I2C通信设置、电压精度及采样要求。 **1. 0.75V电源由哪颗芯片产生？** - **TPS62872**：用于产生VCC0V75，为EQ6的内核供电【11†L4-L5】【14†L22-L24】。 - **SA23002**：用于产生VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR，为EQ6的IO口供电【12†L2-L3】【13†L4-L5】。 **2. 该芯片的输入是什么？** - 两颗芯片的输入均为 **VCC3V3**【11†L4-L5】【13†L4-L5】【14†L22-L24】。 **3. 文档中对该电源的配置要求有哪些？** - **使能控制**： - TPS62872（VCC0V75）由MCU的 **P22.0** 引脚（网络名：MCU_VCC0V75_EN）控制使能，高电平使能，低电平关断【18†L18-L24】。该引脚上电默认需配置为高阻态，且硬件外部下拉【18†L23】。 - SA23002（VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR）由MCU的 **P22.1** 引脚（网络名：MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN）控制使能，高电平使能，低电平关断【12†L5-L6】【18†L18-L24】。该引脚上电默认需配置为高阻态，且硬件外部下拉【12†L6】。 - **I2C通信配置**（仅TPS62872）： - 通过MCU的 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 和 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 引脚进行I2C通信，设置输出电压和通信【11†L9-L10】【14†L22-L24】。 - **电压精度与采样要求**： - VCC0V75（TPS62872输出）的电压精度要求为 **0.75V ± 3%**【17†L8-L9】。 - VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR（SA23002输出）的电压精度要求为 **0.75V ± 5%**【16†L2-L3】。 - MCU通过ADC采样脚对两路电压进行监控，采样计算方式为：`U0V75 = 3.3 * ADC值 / 1024`，并要求硬件滤波截止频率≥121.7Hz，滤波时间≤1.6ms【16†L8-L9】【17†L7-L8】。 - **电路参数要求**（仅TPS62872，来自器件手册）： - 输入电压VIN范围：**2.7V 至 6V**【20†L3-L4】。 - 输出电压范围：**0.4V 至 3.35V**【20†L5-L7】。 - 输出电流能力：最大 **12A**（对应TPS62872-Q1型号）【20†L10-L12】。 - 电感量要求：**110nH 至 330nH**；若开关频率≥2.25MHz且输出电压≤1.675V，电感量范围为 **55nH 至 330nH**【20†L13-L15】。
评分正文：0.75V电源由两颗独立的芯片产生，分别是**TPS62872**（产生VCC0V75）和**SA23002**（产生VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR），两者的输入均为VCC3V3。文档中对应的配置要求包括使能控制、I2C通信设置、电压精度及采样要求。 **1. 0.75V电源由哪颗芯片产生？** - **TPS62872**：用于产生VCC0V75，为EQ6的内核供电【11†L4-L5】【14†L22-L24】。 - **SA23002**：用于产生VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR，为EQ6的IO口供电【12†L2-L3】【13†L4-L5】。 **2. 该芯片的输入是什么？** - 两颗芯片的输入均为 **VCC3V3**【11†L4-L5】【13†L4-L5】【14†L22-L24】。 **3. 文档中对该电源的配置要求有哪些？** - **使能控制**： - TPS62872（VCC0V75）由MCU的 **P22.0** 引脚（网络名：MCU_VCC0V75_EN）控制使能，高电平使能，低电平关断【18†L18-L24】。该引脚上电默认需配置为高阻态，且硬件外部下拉【18†L23】。 - SA23002（VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR）由MCU的 **P22.1** 引脚（网络名：MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN）控制使能，高电平使能，低电平关断【12†L5-L6】【18†L18-L24】。该引脚上电默认需配置为高阻态，且硬件外部下拉【12†L6】。 - **I2C通信配置**（仅TPS62872）： - 通过MCU的 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 和 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 引脚进行I2C通信，设置输出电压和通信【11†L9-L10】【14†L22-L24】。 - **电压精度与采样要求**： - VCC0V75（TPS62872输出）的电压精度要求为 **0.75V ± 3%**【17†L8-L9】。 - VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR（SA23002输出）的电压精度要求为 **0.75V ± 5%**【16†L2-L3】。 - MCU通过ADC采样脚对两路电压进行监控，采样计算方式为：`U0V75 = 3.3 * ADC值 / 1024`，并要求硬件滤波截止频率≥121.7Hz，滤波时间≤1.6ms【16†L8-L9】【17†L7-L8】。 - **电路参数要求**（仅TPS62872，来自器件手册）： - 输入电压VIN范围：**2.7V 至 6V**【20†L3-L4】。 - 输出电压范围：**0.4V 至 3.35V**【20†L5-L7】。 - 输出电流能力：最大 **12A**（对应TPS62872-Q1型号）【20†L10-L12】。 - 电感量要求：**110nH 至 330nH**；若开关频率≥2.25MHz且输出电压≤1.675V，电感量范围为 **55nH 至 330nH**【20†L13-L15】。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. IPC-7525 may have alternate6. design recommendations. | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | The peak-to-peak inductor current ripple is given by: 3.3 - 0.75 2.342A inductors from other manufacturers can also be suitable. 10.2.2.2 Selecting the Input Capacitors As with all buck converters, the input current of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices is discontinuous. The input capacitors provide a low-impedance energy source for the device, and their value, type, and location are critical for correct operation. Tl recommends low-ESR multilayer ceramic capacitors for best performance. In practice, the total Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 input capacitance is typically comprised of a combination of different capacitors, in which larger capacitors provide the decoupling at lower frequencies and smaller capacitors provide the decoupling at higher frequencies. The TPS6287x-Q1 devices feature a butterfly layout with two pairs of VIN and GND pins on opposite sides of the package. This allows the input capacitors to be placed symmetrically on the PCB so that the electromagnetic fields generated cancel each other out, thereby reducing EMl. The duty cycle of the converter is given by: VoUT D = xVT where · Vin is the input voltage. · Vout is the output voltage. · n is the efficiency. The value of input capacitance needed to meet the input voltage ripple requirements is given by: where · D is the duty cycle. · fsw is the switching frequency. · L is the inductance. · lout is the output current. 100mV is used as the input voltage ripple target. The value of Cin calculated with Equation 9 is the effective capacitance after all derating, tolerance, and aging effects have been considered. 5 μF effective capacitance per input pin is required. Tl recommends multilayer ceramic capacitors with an X7R dielectric (or similar) for Cin, and these capacitors must be placed as close to the VIN and GND pins as possible to minimize the loop area. capacitors from other manufacturers can also be suitable. 10.2.2.3 Selecting the Compensation Resistor Use Equation 11 to calculate the recommended value of compensation resistor, Rz: Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. The current limit in practice is given by: where: · I, is the inductor current. ·ILiMH is the high-side current limit threshold measured at DC Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | 9.4 Device Functional Modes 9.4.1 Power-On Reset The device operates in POR mode when the supply voltage is less than the POR threshold, 1.4 V (typical). In POR mode, no functions are available and the content of the device registers is not valid. The device leaves POR mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage increases above the POR threshold 9.4.2 Undervoltage Lockout The device operates in UVLO mode when the supply voltage is between the POR and UVLO thresholds. If the device enters UVLO mode from POR mode, no functions are available. If the device enters UVLO mode from standby mode, the output discharge function is available. The content of the device registers is valid in UVLO mode. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters POR mode when the supply voltage decreases below the POR threshold. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters standby mode when the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold. 9.4.3 Standby The device operates in standby mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold (and the device has completed its initialization) and any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register.· The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. The device initializes for 4o0 μs (typical) after the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold voltage following a device power-on reset. If the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold but not below the POR threshold, the device does not reinitialize when the supply voltage increases again. During initialization, the device reads the state of the FSEL, VSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. The following functions are available in standby mode: · I2C interface ·Output discharge ·Power good Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The device leaves standby mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves standby mode and enters on mode when all of the following conditions are true: · A high-level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. | all operating conditions. 问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：根据现有证据，关于“3.3V电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？”回答如下： ### 结论 **存在**针对特定3.3V网络（VCC3V3_MCU）的硬件过压保护电路，其实现方式是通过一个独立的硬件模块（F18）进行监控和保护。对于系统内其他3.3V电源（如VCC3V3， VCC3V3_EQ, VCC3V3_ETH），现有证据**未明确描述**其输出端具有过压保护功能。 ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 3.3V电源的设计文档中是否描述了过压保护功能？ **是**。文档明确描述了针对MCU的3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）的过压保护功能。 * **来源**：文档[4] 的模块功能列表中将“F18”模块定义为“overvoltage protect”，其功能描述为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。 * **来源**：文档[6] 明确说明：“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。” #### 2. 如果存在过压保护，其在电路设计中的具体实现方式是什么？ **存在**，其实现方式是基于一个带有基准电压和MOSFET（金属氧化物半导体场效应晶体管）开关的电路。 * **来源**：文档[6] 详细描述了模块F18（3V3过压保护电路）的实现方案，具体工作逻辑如下： 1. 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V且高于NMOS管Q1701的VGS（栅源电压）阈值时，NMOS和PMOS（P沟道MOSFET）导通，VCC3V3_SW（受保护的3.3V输出）与VCC3V3直通。 2. 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，电路的cathode引脚输出逻辑低电平，从而关闭NMOS和PMOS，切断VCC3V3_SW对后级负载的供电，实现过压保护。 3. 当VCC3V3电压低于Q1701的VGS阈值时，NMOS和PMOS均关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电。 ### 缺失信息 * **对于系统主电源VCC3V3（由F12模块LN10046产生）**：文档[2]和[3] 描述了该电源的转换方案，但**未提及**其输出端集成了过压保护功能。文档[2] 提到的“双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”是针对输入电源KL30的EMI电路，并非针对VCC3V3输出的过压保护。 * **对于其他由VCC3V3衍生出的3.3V电源（如VCC3V3_EQ, VCC3V3_ETH）**：文档[2] 描述了其通过负载开关（如TPS22918）进行供电管理，但**未提及**这些输出具有过压保护功能。
评分正文：根据现有证据，关于“3.3V电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？”回答如下： ### 结论 **存在**针对特定3.3V网络（VCC3V3_MCU）的硬件过压保护电路，其实现方式是通过一个独立的硬件模块（F18）进行监控和保护。对于系统内其他3.3V电源（如VCC3V3， VCC3V3_EQ, VCC3V3_ETH），现有证据**未明确描述**其输出端具有过压保护功能。 ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 3.3V电源的设计文档中是否描述了过压保护功能？ **是**。文档明确描述了针对MCU的3.3V电源（VCC3V3_MCU）的过压保护功能。 * **来源**：文档[4] 的模块功能列表中将“F18”模块定义为“overvoltage protect”，其功能描述为“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。 * **来源**：文档[6] 明确说明：“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。” #### 2. 如果存在过压保护，其在电路设计中的具体实现方式是什么？ **存在**，其实现方式是基于一个带有基准电压和MOSFET（金属氧化物半导体场效应晶体管）开关的电路。 * **来源**：文档[6] 详细描述了模块F18（3V3过压保护电路）的实现方案，具体工作逻辑如下： 1. 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V且高于NMOS管Q1701的VGS（栅源电压）阈值时，NMOS和PMOS（P沟道MOSFET）导通，VCC3V3_SW（受保护的3.3V输出）与VCC3V3直通。 2. 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，电路的cathode引脚输出逻辑低电平，从而关闭NMOS和PMOS，切断VCC3V3_SW对后级负载的供电，实现过压保护。 3. 当VCC3V3电压低于Q1701的VGS阈值时，NMOS和PMOS均关闭，VCC3V3_SW无电。 ### 缺失信息 * **对于系统主电源VCC3V3（由F12模块LN10046产生）**：文档[2]和[3] 描述了该电源的转换方案，但**未提及**其输出端集成了过压保护功能。文档[2] 提到的“双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护”是针对输入电源KL30的EMI电路，并非针对VCC3V3输出的过压保护。 * **对于其他由VCC3V3衍生出的3.3V电源（如VCC3V3_EQ, VCC3V3_ETH）**：文档[2] 描述了其通过负载开关（如TPS22918）进行供电管理，但**未提及**这些输出具有过压保护功能。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F12</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 注：使用本模板时请先阅读填写说明页，形成工作产品时本页不可删除变更历史Change History目 录Table of Contents1 概述 Introduction 81.1 目的 Purpose 81.2 适用范围 Range of Application 81.3 读者对象 TReader 81.4 术语及缩写解释 Terms and Abbreviations 81.5 参考文档 Reference Document 82 硬件架构 Hardware Architecture 92.1 系统概述 System overview 92.2 硬件框图 Hardware block diagram 102.3 模块电路 Module circuit 122.3.1 KL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit 122.3.2 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit 132.3.3 电源电路 power circuit 152.3.4 TC377最小系统 TC377 minimum system 232.3.5 EQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system 252.3.6 摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system 282.3.7 看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit 302.3.8 温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit 302.3.9 系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuit 312.3.10 ETH电路 ETH circuit 332.3.11 CAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit 342.3.12 3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit 353 硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode 363.1 硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode 363.2 运行模式定义 Definition of Operation Mode 373.3 运行模式转换条件 Transition Condition of Operation Mode 393.3.1 KL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit 393.3.2 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit 413.3.3 电源电路 Power Circuit 423.3.4 TC377最小系统 TC377 minimum system 53 | None：MCU采样摄像头的1V8电源电压，精度要求：1.7~1.9V; </td><td></td><td >92</td><td >94</td><td >96</td><td >%VoUT </td></tr> <tr><td >VT+</td><td > Positive-going power good threshold voltage (output overvoltage)</td><td></td><td >104</td><td </td></tr> <tr><th >VoL</th><th >Low-level output voltage (PG)</th><th >loL = 1 mA</th><th></th><th></th><th >0.3</th><th></th></tr> </table> | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. hw-v1-circuit-tps22918-enumerationsuccess0.5380.5910.667not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.250not_applicable
问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：基于证据，板上共有3个TPS22918实例，分别对应三个电源开关。以下是每个实例的输入、输出和使能信息： **直接结论：** - **U1500**：使能网络为MCU_3V3_ETH_EN，输入和输出网络在证据中未直接说明。 - **U1802**：使能网络为MCU_3V3_EQ_EN，输入和输出网络在证据中未直接说明。 - **U1803**：使能网络为MCU_1V8_EQ_EN，输入和输出网络在证据中未直接说明。 **支撑证据：** 1. **实例U1500**：原理图中标识为TPS22918（证据[10]），其使能引脚（ON）连接至网络MCU_3V3_ETH_EN（证据[8]）。关于输入（VIN）和输出（VOUT）连接的网络，在提供的电路证据中未直接说明。 2. **实例U1802**：原理图中标识为TPS22918（证据[11]），其使能引脚（ON）连接至网络MCU_3V3_EQ_EN（证据[7]）。关于输入（VIN）和输出（VOUT）连接的网络，在提供的电路证据中未直接说明。 3. **实例U1803**：原理图中标识为TPS22918（证据[12]），其使能引脚（ON）连接至网络MCU_1V8_EQ_EN（证据[6]）。关于输入（VIN）和输出（VOUT）连接的网络，在提供的电路证据中未直接说明。 **缺失信息：** - 每个TPS22918实例的输入（VIN）和输出（VOUT）所连接的具体网络名称，在提供的电路设计证据中未直接给出。

问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？

问题：U1700 的替代料和采购单价是多少？

问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：主控 MCU 型号为 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”中明确描述：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”以及“根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。（来源：证据[10] document:87e0c61422f91bc3） **缺失信息**：无。所有需要的信息均已覆盖。
评分正文：主控 MCU 型号为 **SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**。 **支撑证据**： - 文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”中明确描述：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”以及“根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA”。（来源：证据[10] document:87e0c61422f91bc3） **缺失信息**：无。所有需要的信息均已覆盖。

OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：你好，你能做什么？

问题：板上最主要的电源管理芯片是谁？说清楚输入、三路输出和软件配置出处。
参考答案：U1700 LP87702-Q1 从 VCC3V3 取电，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50，软件配置出处为 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：**结论：板上最主要的电源管理芯片（三路输出）是 LP87702。** **输入：** - 输入电压为 VCC3V3（由系统一级电源 LN10046 将 KL30 12V 转换而来）。 **来源：** 文档[11]中描述“LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出”。 **三路输出：** - 输出电压分别为 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50。 **来源：** 同上，文档[11]明确列出三路输出。 **软件配置出处：** - MCU 通过 I2C 总线配置 LP87702 的寄存器，具体寄存器配置表为文档中的“Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。 **来源：** 文档[8]指出“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”；文档[11]也说明“MCU可以通过I2C监控和管理LP87702的状态”。 **注意：** - 系统一级电源 LN10046（KL30→VCC3V3）虽为第一级电源，但仅有一路输出，不符合“三路输出”要求，故不选为答案。 - 证据中未提供 LP87702 的具体寄存器地址或配置值，仅指出出处为 Table 5-2，但该表格内容未在检索结果中完整呈现。

Power off mode After the enable signal ECU_EN=L, the primary power LN10046 stops the power supply of VCC3V3, and the system stops working. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F17</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH is used for ETH power supply, and the load switching scheme is now selected.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_ETH，提供给MCU的ETH供电。P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-ETH to supply power to ETH of the MCU. Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Tl's customers are responsible for determining suitability of components for their purposes, as well as validating and testing their design implementation to confirm systemfunctionality. 10.1 Application Information The following section discusses selection of the external components to complete the power supply design for typical a application. 10.2 Typical Application 10.2.1 Design Requirements Preliminary Calculations The maximum allowable deviation of the power supply is ±3.3%. The DC accuracy of the TPS6287x-Q1 is specified as ±1%, therefore, the maximum output voltage variation during a transient is given by: VoUT = ± VouT × (3.3% - 1%) = ±17.25 mV 10.2.2 Detailed Design Procedure The following subsections describe how to calculate the external components required to meet the specified transient requirements of a given application. The calculations include the worst-case variation of components and use the RMS method to combine the variation of uncorrelated parameters. 10.2.2.1 Selecting the Inductor The TPS6287x-Q1 devices have been optimized for inductors in the range 50 nH to 300 nH. If the transient response of the converter is limited by the slew rate of the current in the inductor, using a smaller inductor can improve performance. However, the output ripple current increases as the value of the inductor decreases and higher output current ripple generates higher output voltage ripple, which adds to the transient overshoot or undershoot. The optimal configuration for a given application is always a trade-off between a number of parameters. Tl recommends a starting value of 110 nH for typical applications. | The peak-to-peak inductor current ripple is given by: 3.3 - 0.75 2.342A inductors from other manufacturers can also be suitable. 10.2.2.2 Selecting the Input Capacitors As with all buck converters, the input current of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices is discontinuous. The input capacitors provide a low-impedance energy source for the device, and their value, type, and location are critical for correct operation. Tl recommends low-ESR multilayer ceramic capacitors for best performance. In practice, the total Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 input capacitance is typically comprised of a combination of different capacitors, in which larger capacitors provide the decoupling at lower frequencies and smaller capacitors provide the decoupling at higher frequencies. The TPS6287x-Q1 devices feature a butterfly layout with two pairs of VIN and GND pins on opposite sides of the package. This allows the input capacitors to be placed symmetrically on the PCB so that the electromagnetic fields generated cancel each other out, thereby reducing EMl. The duty cycle of the converter is given by: VoUT D = xVT where · Vin is the input voltage. · Vout is the output voltage. · n is the efficiency. The value of input capacitance needed to meet the input voltage ripple requirements is given by: where · D is the duty cycle. · fsw is the switching frequency. · L is the inductance. · lout is the output current. 100mV is used as the input voltage ripple target. The value of Cin calculated with Equation 9 is the effective capacitance after all derating, tolerance, and aging effects have been considered. 5 μF effective capacitance per input pin is required. Tl recommends multilayer ceramic capacitors with an X7R dielectric (or similar) for Cin, and these capacitors must be placed as close to the VIN and GND pins as possible to minimize the loop area. capacitors from other manufacturers can also be suitable. 10.2.2.3 Selecting the Compensation Resistor Use Equation 11 to calculate the recommended value of compensation resistor, Rz: Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. The current limit in practice is given by: where: · I, is the inductor current. ·ILiMH is the high-side current limit threshold measured at DC Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | td(RAMP) = 500 μs td(RAMP) = 1 ms (default) td(RAMP) = 2 ms td(RAMP) = 4 ms· The device ignores new values until the soft-start sequence is complete if the user programs the following when the device soft-start sequence has already started: A new output voltage setpoint (VOuT[7:o]) An output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]) Soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings If the user change the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, the device ramps up or down to the new value. The device can start up into a prebiased output. In this case, only a portion of the internal voltage ramp is seen externally (see Figure 9-9). Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据电路设计文件（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中U1800的引脚映射，其输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络如下： - **输入网络**：VIN（连接至 **VCC3V3**） - **输出检测网络**：VOSNS（连接至 **VCC0V75**）和 GOSNS（连接至 **GND**） - **使能网络**：EN（连接至 **N19888813**） - **I²C控制网络**：SDA（连接至 **MCU_SDA_P15.5**）和 SCL（连接至 **MCU_SCL_P15.4**） **支撑证据**： - 电路设计文件中的引脚映射（`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`）明确列出了上述所有引脚及其网络连接。 - 实例信息（`circuit:21:instance:U1800`）确认器件型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，与引脚映射一致。 **补充说明**： - 文档资料（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中提及“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片”，但当前引脚映射中EN的网络为N19888813，未直接显示该网络与MCU P22.0的连接关系，故不将其作为结论。 - 证据中未发现任何冲突。
评分正文：根据电路设计文件（825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）中U1800的引脚映射，其输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络如下： - **输入网络**：VIN（连接至 **VCC3V3**） - **输出检测网络**：VOSNS（连接至 **VCC0V75**）和 GOSNS（连接至 **GND**） - **使能网络**：EN（连接至 **N19888813**） - **I²C控制网络**：SDA（连接至 **MCU_SDA_P15.5**）和 SCL（连接至 **MCU_SCL_P15.4**） **支撑证据**： - 电路设计文件中的引脚映射（`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`）明确列出了上述所有引脚及其网络连接。 - 实例信息（`circuit:21:instance:U1800`）确认器件型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，与引脚映射一致。 **补充说明**： - 文档资料（600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）中提及“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片”，但当前引脚映射中EN的网络为N19888813，未直接显示该网络与MCU P22.0的连接关系，故不将其作为结论。 - 证据中未发现任何冲突。

Acknowledge on the I²c Bus Data Output by Transmitter Not Acknowledge Data Output by Receiver Acknowledge SCL from Controller START Clock pulse for acknowledgment Condition | In standalone (nonstacked) applications, the user can disable the active pulldown of the EN pin if the user sets SINGLE = 1 in the CONTROL3 register. Fault conditions have no effect on the EN pin when SINGLE = 1 (the EN pin is always pulled down during device initialization). In stacked applications, ensure that SINGLE = 0. When the internal SYSTEM_READY signal is low (that is, initialization is complete and there are no fault conditions), the internal open-drain transistor is high impedance and the EN pin functions like a standard input. A high level on the EN pin enables the DC/DC converter in the device. A low level disables the DC/DC converter (the I2C interface is enabled as soon as the device has completed its initialization and is not affected by the state of the internal ENABLE or SYSTEM_READY signals). A low level on the EN pin forces the device into shutdown. During shutdown, the MOSFETs in the power stage are off, the internal control circuitry is disabled, and the device consumes only 20 μA (typical). The rising threshold voltage of the EN pin is 1.0 V and the falling threshold voltage is 0.9 V. The tolerance of the threshold voltages is ±30 mV, which means that the user can use the EN pin to implement precise turn-on and turn-off behavior. When power is applied to the VIN pin, the toggling of the EN pin does not reset the loaded default register settings. 9.3.4 Start-Up When the voltage on the VIN pin exceeds the positive-going UVLO threshold, the device initializes as follows: ·The device pulls the EN pin low. ·The device enables the internal reference voltage. · The device reads the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. · The device loads the default values into the device registers. When initialization is complete, the device enables I²C communication and releases the EN pin. The external circuitry controlling the EN pin now determines the behavior of the device: · If the EN pin is low, the device is disabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers, but the DC/DC converter does not operate · If the EN pin is high, the device is enabled. The user can write to and read from the device registers and, after a short delay, the DC/DC converter starts to ramp up its output. The SSTIME[1:0] bits in the CONTROL2 register select the duration of the soft-start ramp: | The image displays an electronic circuit diagram centered around a rectangular integrated circuit (IC) component with multiple labeled pins on its left and right sides. Address Selection (1)Available I?C address. Refer to the Section 6</caption> <tr><td >VSEL Pin</td><td >12C Target Address (1)</td></tr> <tr><td >6.2 kQ to GND</td><td >0x40 or0x30</td></tr> <tr><td >Short Circuit to GND</td><td >0x41 or0x31</td></tr> <tr><td > Short Circuit to VIN</td><td >0x42or 0x32</td></tr> <tr><td >47 kQ to VIn </td><td >0x43 or 0x33</td></tr> </table> | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | <table><caption> over operating junction temperature (TJ = -40 °C to 150 °C) and Vin = 2.7 V to 6 V. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：**结论：** VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **U1803（TPS22918）**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚控制）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **负载开关型号及路径** - 证据[1]（电路原理图 pin_mapping）显示 U1803 的 VIN 接 VCC1V8，VOUT 接 VCC1V8_EQ，QOD 也接 VCC1V8_EQ。 - 证据[18]（实例）确认 U1803 的器件型号为 TPS22918。 - 证据[15]（net VCC1V8_EQ）连接了 U1803 的 VOUT 和 QOD，以及 EQ 相关供电引脚。 2. **使能信号** - 证据[1] 中 U1803 的 ON 引脚映射为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。 - 证据[10]（net MCU_1V8_EQ_EN）显示该网络连接了 U1803.ON 和 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚（U900E.P20.0）。 - 文档证据[19] 和 [20] 描述：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ … 用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片”，与原理图一致。 **缺口说明：** - 检索账本中子问题 sq_2（使能信号）的状态被标记为“missing”，但电路原理图证据[1]和[10]已直接给出使能网络名及其来源，此处无实际信息缺口。
评分正文：**结论：** VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过的负载开关是 **U1803（TPS22918）**，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**（由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚控制）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **负载开关型号及路径** - 证据[1]（电路原理图 pin_mapping）显示 U1803 的 VIN 接 VCC1V8，VOUT 接 VCC1V8_EQ，QOD 也接 VCC1V8_EQ。 - 证据[18]（实例）确认 U1803 的器件型号为 TPS22918。 - 证据[15]（net VCC1V8_EQ）连接了 U1803 的 VOUT 和 QOD，以及 EQ 相关供电引脚。 2. **使能信号** - 证据[1] 中 U1803 的 ON 引脚映射为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**。 - 证据[10]（net MCU_1V8_EQ_EN）显示该网络连接了 U1803.ON 和 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚（U900E.P20.0）。 - 文档证据[19] 和 [20] 描述：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ … 用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片”，与原理图一致。 **缺口说明：** - 检索账本中子问题 sq_2（使能信号）的状态被标记为“missing”，但电路原理图证据[1]和[10]已直接给出使能网络名及其来源，此处无实际信息缺口。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.
