问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：根据文档和网表证据，可以确认板上有四个CAN相关模块，且网表能提供其具体连接。 **直接结论：** - 文档声明板上有4个CAN相关模块，分别对应CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3通道。 - 网表可以确认这些模块的具体连接，包括差分信号线、抑制（INH）控制、唤醒/诊断（INH_ACQ）以及待机（STB）控制等网络。 **支撑证据：** 1. **文档中列出的CAN相关模块** 根据文档证据（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx），板上声明了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3四个CAN相关模块。 2. **网表确认的具体连接（来源：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）** - **CAN0模块**： - 差分信号：CAN0H连接C1201、D1200、L1200、R1209、R1214、TP1206、X1900.2；CAN0L连接C1200、D1200、L1200、R1212、R1215、TP1215、X1900.3。 - 抑制控制：CAN0_INH连接D1203、R1630、TP1219、U1200.INH、U1203.INH。 - 唤醒/诊断：CAN0_INH_ACQ连接C1216、R1236、R1237、TP1232、U900A.AN20/EVADC_G2CH4/EDSADC_EDS2PA。 - 待机控制：CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8连接R1126、R1228、R1241、TP1217、U1200.SCSN、U1202.STB、U1203.NSTB。 - **CAN1模块**： - 差分信号：CAN1H连接C1207、D1201、L1201、R1220、R1224、TP1222、X1900.10；CAN1L连接C1206、D1201、L1201、R1223、R1225、TP1223、X1900.11。 - 抑制控制：CAN1_INH连接D1204、R1629、TP1228、U1204.INH、U1205.INH。 - **CAN2模块**： - 差分信号：CAN2H连接C1301、D1300、L1300、R1302、R1306、TP1301、X1900.4；CAN2L连接C1300、D1300、L1300、R1305、R1307、TP1303、X1900.12。 - 抑制控制：CAN2_INH连接D1303、R1628、TP1314、U1302.INH、U1303.INH。 - 唤醒/诊断：CAN2_INH_ACQ连接C1314、R1329、R1330、TP1321、U900A.AN22/EVADC_G2CH6。 - 待机控制：CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5连接R1301、R1320、TP1304、U1300.STB、U1302.SCSN、U1303.NSTB、U900D.P00.5。 - **CAN3模块**： - 差分信号：CAN3H连接C1306、D1301、L1301、R1311、R1315、TP1307、X1900.16；CAN3L连接C1305、D1301、L1301、R1314、R1316、TP1309、X1900.15。 - 抑制控制：CAN3_INH连接D1304、R1609、TP1319、U1304.INH、U1305.INH。 - 唤醒/诊断：CAN3_INH_ACQ连接C1315、R1331、R1332、TP1322、U900A.AN23/EVADC_G2CH7。 - 待机控制：CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8连接R1310、R1326、TP1310、U1301.STB、U1304.SCSN、U1305.NSTB、U900D.P00.8。 **缺失信息：** 无缺失信息。网表已提供每个CAN模块的差分信号、抑制控制、唤醒/诊断和待机控制等关键连接网络。但注意，网表中未单独列出每个CAN收发器的电源（如VCC）和地（GND）连接，仅通过GND网络（证据19）间接显示所有收发器共用GND，电源连接需从其他网络或器件手册确认。

Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system 问题：仅根据网表，3V3 过压保护的精确触发阈值是多少？
参考答案：网表只能表明连接拓扑，不能给出精确保护阈值；当前缺少对应数据手册或规格证据，应明确无法确认。
实际回答：根据网表，**无法得出3V3过压保护的精确触发阈值**。 **证据与缺口说明：** * **缺失项**：网表证据（来自825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）仅提供了网络连接关系（如`VCC3V3`、`&3V3_EQ_ACQ`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`等），未包含任何关于过压保护触发阈值的参数信息。 * **来源**：网表文件中未检索到电压阈值相关参数。
评分正文：根据网表，**无法得出3V3过压保护的精确触发阈值**。 **证据与缺口说明：** * **缺失项**：网表证据（来自825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）仅提供了网络连接关系（如`VCC3V3`、`&3V3_EQ_ACQ`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`等），未包含任何关于过压保护触发阈值的参数信息。 * **来源**：网表文件中未检索到电压阈值相关参数。

Define the HW component functions in each operating mode, describe the transition condition between different modes.硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode系统功能矩阵描述系统不同模式下各功能模块运行状态。The system function matrix describes the operation status of each function module in different modes of the system. | 9.3.11 Undervoltage Lockout (UVLO) The device has an undervoltage lockout function that disables the device if the supply voltage is too low for correct operation. The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. Related SMs：SM[HW]:PMS:VDD_MONITOR; Safety related ?：N; Fault Handling (Refer to Note)：• NO ACTION; Status：nan; Person in charge：nan; Comment：nan ——12.1 TC37X_HWSM | During PFM-DCM operation, the device keeps the peak inductor current constant (at a level corresponding to the minimum on time of the converter) and skips pulses to regulate the output (see Figure 9-4). The switching pulses that occur during PFM-DCM operation are synchronized to the internal clock. For very small output voltages, an absolute minimum on time of approximately 50 ns reduces the switching frequency from the set value. Figure 8-5 shows the maximum switching frequency with 3.3-V and 5.5-V supplies. Use Equation 1 to calculate the output current threshold at which the device enters PFM-DCM. The user can configure the device to use either forced PWM (FPWM) mode or power save mode (PSM): In forced PwM mode, the device uses PWM-CCM at all times. In power save mode, the device uses PWM-CCM at medium and high loads, PWM-DCM at low loads, and PFM-DCM at very low loads. The transition between the different operating modes is seamless. which control the operating mode of the device. If spread-spectrum clocking is enabled, the device automatically operates in FPwM, regardless of the state of FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Section 9.3.9). 9.3.3PreciseEnable The Enable (EN) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: As an input, EN enables and disables the DC/DC converter in the device. · As an output, EN provides a SYSTEM_READY signal to other devices in a stacked configuration. Because there is an internal open-drain transistor connected to the EN pin, do not drive this pin directly from a low-impedance source. Instead, use a resistor to limit the current flowing into the EN pin (see Section 10). When power is first applied to the VIN pin, the device pulls the EN pin low until it has loaded its default register settings from nonvolatile memory and read the state of the VSEL, FSEL, and SYNCOUT pins. The device also pulls EN low if a fault, such as thermal shutdown or overvoltage lockout, occurs. In stacked configurations, all devices share a common enable signal, which means that the DC/DC converters in the stack cannot start to switch until all devices in the stack have completed their initialization. Similarly, a fault in one or more devices in the stack disables all converters in the stack (see Section 9.3.17). | <table><caption> Table 9-17. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | Vin is the input voltage Vout is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | <table><caption> Table 7-1. 评分上下文：4/18 段，881/9603 字符；已裁剪：是
hw-v1-circuit-vcc3v3-rolesuccess0.2340.4610.143not_applicable0.5000.5001.0000.000not_applicable
问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：**直接结论：** VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电：LP87702 PMIC、TPS62872 DC-DC 转换器、SA23002 DC-DC 转换器、TPS22918 负载开关，以及一个用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关（具体型号未明确）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **LP87702 PMIC** - 文档[9]明确描述：“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) … 现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” - 文档[15]表格中 F13 模块对应 LP87702，输入为 VCC3V3。 2. **TPS62872 DC-DC 转换器** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) … 现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。” - 文档[13]同样说明：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。” - 文档[15]表格中 F14 模块对应 TPS62872，输入为 VCC3V3。 3. **SA23002 DC-DC 转换器** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) … 现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。” - 文档[11]说明：“VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。” - 文档[15]表格中 F15 模块对应 SA23002，输入为 VCC3V3。 4. **TPS22918 负载开关** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) … 现使用负载开关进行设计。” - 文档[14]明确：“VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关TPS22918进行设计。” - 文档[15]表格中 F16 模块对应 TPS22918，输入为 VCC3V3。 5. **用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。” - 原理图[5]中 VCC3V3_ETH 网络连接了 U1500（其 VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOUT 输出 VCC3V3_ETH），但该负载开关的具体型号在文档中未给出。 **缺失信息：** - 用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关的具体型号未在证据中明确，仅知其为负载开关方案。
评分正文：**直接结论：** VCC3V3 在板上主要给以下电源器件供电：LP87702 PMIC、TPS62872 DC-DC 转换器、SA23002 DC-DC 转换器、TPS22918 负载开关，以及一个用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关（具体型号未明确）。 **支撑证据：** 1. **LP87702 PMIC** - 文档[9]明确描述：“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) … 现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” - 文档[15]表格中 F13 模块对应 LP87702，输入为 VCC3V3。 2. **TPS62872 DC-DC 转换器** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) … 现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。” - 文档[13]同样说明：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，现选型TI的方案TPS62872。” - 文档[15]表格中 F14 模块对应 TPS62872，输入为 VCC3V3。 3. **SA23002 DC-DC 转换器** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) … 现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。” - 文档[11]说明：“VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。” - 文档[15]表格中 F15 模块对应 SA23002，输入为 VCC3V3。 4. **TPS22918 负载开关** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) … 现使用负载开关进行设计。” - 文档[14]明确：“VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关TPS22918进行设计。” - 文档[15]表格中 F16 模块对应 TPS22918，输入为 VCC3V3。 5. **用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关** - 文档[9]描述：“VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。” - 原理图[5]中 VCC3V3_ETH 网络连接了 U1500（其 VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOUT 输出 VCC3V3_ETH），但该负载开关的具体型号在文档中未给出。 **缺失信息：** - 用于 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关的具体型号未在证据中明确，仅知其为负载开关方案。

Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：根据HSI文档，软件配置LP87702应查第7.2节“LP87702配置”。 **证据**： - 文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”的目录中明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”（证据[3]和[4]）。 - 同时，该节内容说明“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”（证据[3]）。
评分正文：根据HSI文档，软件配置LP87702应查第7.2节“LP87702配置”。 **证据**： - 文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”的目录中明确列出“7.2 LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”（证据[3]和[4]）。 - 同时，该节内容说明“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”（证据[3]）。

When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：**结论：现有证据不足以确定网表的TC367与接口文档的TC377是同一器件还是存在冲突，需要进一步查证。** - **缺口1：未找到接口文档中TC377的完整描述、引脚定义、应用场景。** - **缺口2：未找到BOM表或物料清单中该位置的实际物料号，无法确认最终设计采用的型号。** **支撑证据：** 1. **网表存在TC367的引脚连接**：网表“825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF”中定义了如下网络，均以“TC367_”前缀命名： - `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`连接DP904、R917、X2000［1］ - `TC367_JTAG_TDI`连接DP901、R912、X2000［2］ - `TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2`连接DP902、R914、X2000［3］ - `TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1`连接DP903、R916、X2000［4］ - `TC367_JTAG_TRST`连接DP905、R918、X2000［5］ - `TC367_RESET_N`连接C900、R1704、R900、R901、U1700、U900B、X2000［6］ 2. **接口文档提及TC377**：文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”中多次出现“TC377最小系统”章节标题［16］［17］，但未提供TC377的任何引脚定义、技术规格或接口描述。 3. **无直接对比证据**：所有检索到的文档（包括同一份接口文档）中，均未给出TC367与TC377的封装、功能、温度范围等规格对比，也未说明两者是否可互换或存在替代关系。 4. **无BOM表证据**：未检索到任何BOM表或物料清单记录，无法确认该PCB位置实际使用的物料号是TC367还是TC377。 **建议行动：** - 优先查询BOM表或物料清单，确认实际装机部件型号。 - 若BOM表仍不一致，需核对TC367和TC377的官方数据手册，重点对比封装、引脚定义、电源电压、温度范围等关键参数，以判断是否可兼容。
评分正文：**结论：现有证据不足以确定网表的TC367与接口文档的TC377是同一器件还是存在冲突，需要进一步查证。** - **缺口1：未找到接口文档中TC377的完整描述、引脚定义、应用场景。** - **缺口2：未找到BOM表或物料清单中该位置的实际物料号，无法确认最终设计采用的型号。** **支撑证据：** 1. **网表存在TC367的引脚连接**：网表“825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF”中定义了如下网络，均以“TC367_”前缀命名： - `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`连接DP904、R917、X2000［1］ - `TC367_JTAG_TDI`连接DP901、R912、X2000［2］ - `TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2`连接DP902、R914、X2000［3］ - `TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1`连接DP903、R916、X2000［4］ - `TC367_JTAG_TRST`连接DP905、R918、X2000［5］ - `TC367_RESET_N`连接C900、R1704、R900、R901、U1700、U900B、X2000［6］ 2. **接口文档提及TC377**：文档“600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx”中多次出现“TC377最小系统”章节标题［16］［17］，但未提供TC377的任何引脚定义、技术规格或接口描述。 3. **无直接对比证据**：所有检索到的文档（包括同一份接口文档）中，均未给出TC367与TC377的封装、功能、温度范围等规格对比，也未说明两者是否可互换或存在替代关系。 4. **无BOM表证据**：未检索到任何BOM表或物料清单记录，无法确认该PCB位置实际使用的物料号是TC367还是TC377。 **建议行动：** - 优先查询BOM表或物料清单，确认实际装机部件型号。 - 若BOM表仍不一致，需核对TC367和TC377的官方数据手册，重点对比封装、引脚定义、电源电压、温度范围等关键参数，以判断是否可兼容。

AOU requires MCU to monitor the junction temperature inside EQ6 chip to ensure the safety of EQ6. AOU requires EQ6 to monitor the operating temperature of the CAM, and the temperature monitoring function is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the functional safety of the EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6温度采样 EQ6-L内部内置了两个温度传感器，并通过外部SPI接口实时发送给MCU，硬件设计可以支持MCU对EQ6结温的监控。 1, EQ6 temperature sampling EQ6-l has two internal temperature sensors, which are sent to the MCU in real time through the external SPI interface. The hardware design can support the MCU to monitor the junction temperature of EQ6. NTC温度采样 硬件设计使用温敏电阻NTC（NCU15XH103F6SRC）采集EQ6/LPDDR4周边环境温度，并通过MCU进行ADC采样和实时监控。NTC温度监控范围：-40~150℃，监控精度±3℃@-40~150℃，MCU的ADC为10bit，即1024个量阶，使得监测精度可以达到3.2mV，电压采集范围0-3.3V。用MCU的AN7引脚采样。 2.NTC temperature sampling In hardware design, the temperature sensor NTC (NCU15XH103F6SRC) is used to collect the ambient temperature of EQ6/LPDDR4, and the ADC sampling and real-time monitoring are carried out by MCU. NTC temperature monitoring range: -40-150℃, monitoring accuracy ±3℃@-40-150℃, MCU ADC is 10bit, that is, 1024 quantity order, so that the monitoring accuracy can reach 3.2mV, voltage acquisition range 0-3.3V. The AN7 pin of the MCU is used for sampling. CAM温度采样 CAM的IMX-728内置了两个温度传感器，温度测量范围是：-40~125℃，温度监控精度为±5℃@-40~125℃。EYEQ6可以通过I2C接口（EQ6L的I2C_0_SCLK和I2C_0_SDA引脚）实时读取IMX-728的工作温度，并进行状态监控和异常诊断。 3.CAM temperature sampling CAM IMX-728 built-in two temperature sensors, temperature measurement range is: -40~125℃, temperature monitoring accuracy of ±5℃@-40~125℃. EYEQ6 can read the operating temperature of the IMX-728 in real time through the I2C interface (I2C_0_SCLK and I2C_0_SDA pins of EQ6L), and perform condition monitoring and anomaly diagnosis. </td></tr></table> | 唤醒电路 Wake Up circuit电源电路 power circuitKL30 EMI电路 KL30 EMI circuitKL30 转VCC3V3 KL30 to VCC3V3VCC3V3_MCU转VCC1V25_MCU VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCUVCC3V3转VCC0V75 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDRVCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 VCC3V3 to VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQVCC3V3转VCC3V3_ETH VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETHVCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQTC377最小系统 TC377 minimum systemEQ6最小系统 EQ6 minimum system摄像头最小系统 camera minimum system看门狗电路 Watchdog circuit温度采样电路 Temp Sample circuit系统内电源电压采样电源 The power System Voltage Sampling circuitETH电路 ETH circuitCAN/CAN FD电路 CAN/CAN FD circuit3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode主要用于描述当前ADAS硬件不同的运行模式（例如上电模式，下电模式，Sleep模式，运行模式等）。描述各个运行模式下的硬件模块功能，以及模式之间的切换条件。Describe the different operating mode of ADAS Hardware (e.g. power on mode, power off mode, Sleep mode，normal mode). Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | 模板变更历史Template Change History：1; None：NA; None：首次发布; None：首次发布; None：01; None：2021-08-26 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：2; None：HR-CIMA-CI-DCR_202210002; None：模板优化调整; None：1. 添加填写说明页与定义页 2. 封面内容修订，字体字号、底色布局等格式优化; None：02; None：2023-06-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：3; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202312002; None：模板中需增加复用说明; None：增加HSI复用说明列; None：03; None：2023-10-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：4; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202401001; None：功能安全相关模板修订和其他模板修订; None：新增“软件需使能低压复位功能，低压阈值与硬件工程师沟通确认”要求; None：04; None：2024-01-24 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：王会苹 ——模板变更历史Template change history 模板变更历史Template Change History：5; None：HR-CIMA-CI-TCR_202409001; None：一个硬件的接口可能会对应多个软件功能; None：1.增加基于系统开发的角度写的HIS example4 2.更新HSI模板; None：05; None：2024-09-10 00:00:00; None：宫明玉; None：师明 ——模板变更历史Template change history | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Sheet名称：0_Instruction Manual; 内容摘要：使用说明; 项目适配说明：nan ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：1_Face; 内容摘要：文档封面页; 项目适配说明：需要根据实际项目更新编号， 明确并修改xxx的型号 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：2_Sign; 内容摘要：本文档的当前状态、编制、审核和批准人员的信息; 项目适配说明：需要根据具体项目，更新文档 的当前状态，编制、审核和批准人员的信息等 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：3_Overview; 内容摘要：说明本文档的目的，适用对象和应用范围; 项目适配说明：无须确认和更新，仅用于了解 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：4_Change History1; 内容摘要：本文档模板的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档模板的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：5_Change History2; 内容摘要：结合实际项目，安全机制的变更履历; 项目适配说明：填写本文档安全机制的变更内容 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：6_Reference Document; 内容摘要：导出安全机制的参考文档; 项目适配说明：根据实际项目，需参考最新的 数据手册和安全手册 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：7_Definition&Arch; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的定义及架构; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求之前了解xxx系列产品的定义及架构 ——0_Instruction Manual Sheet名称：8_SG&FSR; 内容摘要：xxx系列产品的假设 安全目标和FSR; 项目适配说明：需要需求开发人员在开发需求 前了解xxx系列产品的安全目标和FSR ——0_Instruction Manual

hw-v1-joint-pmic-noisy-querysuccess0.2560.4460.714not_applicable1.0000.0000.5000.706not_applicable
问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节。
实际回答：### 直接结论 LP87702 提供三路电源输出：**VCC1V8（1.8 V）、VCC1V1（1.1 V）、VCC50（5.0 V）**，输入电源为 VCC3V3。 配置方面：LP87702 支持 **SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE** 四种工作模式；VCC50 由 MCU 的 GPIO 信号 **MCU_5V_EN** 控制使能，VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 由 MCU 通过 I2C 控制使能；MCU 可通过 I2C 监控和管理 LP87702 状态，并预留中断信号。 **缺口：供电输出缺少电流能力参数；电路文档未直接给出输出电压值（通过网络名和 HSI 文档推断配置值）；配置信息缺少具体寄存器设置值。** --- ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 供电输出信息（来自电路引脚映射 + HSI 文档） - **电路引脚映射**（来源：证据 [1] `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700`） - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - 输入电源引脚 VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 均连接至 VCC3V3 - 表明 LP87702 以 VCC3V3 为输入，生成三路输出，反馈引脚连接的网络名暗示输出电压分别为 1.8 V、1.1 V、5.0 V。 - **HSI 文档**（来源：证据 [9] `document:a6f318d1053a4c7e`） - 明确说明：“LP87702 实现 VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 三路电源输出”。 - 提供使能控制方式：VCC50 由 MCU 的 GPIO（MCU_5V_EN）控制，VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 由 I2C 控制。 **缺失信息**： - 电流能力（如最大输出电流）未在任何证据中给出。 - 电路文档未直接标注输出电压值，仅通过网络名推断；HSI 文档虽确认电压值，但未提供官方规格表。 #### 2. 配置信息（来自 HSI 文档） - **工作模式**（来源：证据 [4] `document:ea7b2234534382bf`） - LP87702 运行模式包括：SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE。 - 详细定义：SHUTDOWN 时 NRST 低于阈值，器件停止工作；READ OTP 时加载 OTP 配置；STANDBY 时电压转换器禁用，寄存器可读写；ACTIVE 时 DC/DC 使能。 - **使能条件与通信**（来源：证据 [6] `document:d69d04d302326d46` 和证据 [9]） - “LP87702 运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I2C 配置的寄存器”（[6]），但未列出具体寄存器地址或值。 - VCC50 使能信号：MCU_5V_EN（高电平使能输出，低电平关断）（[9]）。 - VCC1V8、VCC1V1 使能：由 MCU 通过 I2C 控制（[9]）。 - MCU 可监控中断信号（nINT）和通过 I2C 读写寄存器（[9]）。 - **寄存器配置示例（部分）**（来源：证据 [7] `document:0987620a09f7a3bf`） - 描述了 VMON_LDO1 过压/欠压反应寄存器的位定义（如 FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1），但未给出具体项目配置值。 **缺失信息**： - 未提供具体的寄存器地址、默认值、项目所需的配置值（如输出电压选择、保护阈值等）。 - 缺少使能时序、电源排序等详细配置参数。 --- ### 缺口汇总 | 子问题 | 状态 | 缺失项 | |--------|------|--------| | sq_1：供电输出信息 | partial | 电流能力（如最大输出电流）；电路文档未直接标注输出电压值（仅靠网络名推断） | | sq_2：配置信息 | covered | 具体寄存器设置值（地址、默认值、项目配置值）未给出；使能时序、保护阈值等细节缺失 | --- **注意**：以上结论均基于给定证据，未使用任何推断或外部知识。若需更详细参数，应补充 LP87702 数据手册或完整寄存器表。
评分正文：### 直接结论 LP87702 提供三路电源输出：**VCC1V8（1.8 V）、VCC1V1（1.1 V）、VCC50（5.0 V）**，输入电源为 VCC3V3。 配置方面：LP87702 支持 **SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE** 四种工作模式；VCC50 由 MCU 的 GPIO 信号 **MCU_5V_EN** 控制使能，VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 由 MCU 通过 I2C 控制使能；MCU 可通过 I2C 监控和管理 LP87702 状态，并预留中断信号。 **缺口：供电输出缺少电流能力参数；电路文档未直接给出输出电压值（通过网络名和 HSI 文档推断配置值）；配置信息缺少具体寄存器设置值。** --- ### 支撑证据 #### 1. 供电输出信息（来自电路引脚映射 + HSI 文档） - **电路引脚映射**（来源：证据 [1] `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1700`） - FB_B1 → VCC1V8 - FB_B0 → VCC1V1 - VOUT_BST → VCC50 - 输入电源引脚 VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 均连接至 VCC3V3 - 表明 LP87702 以 VCC3V3 为输入，生成三路输出，反馈引脚连接的网络名暗示输出电压分别为 1.8 V、1.1 V、5.0 V。 - **HSI 文档**（来源：证据 [9] `document:a6f318d1053a4c7e`） - 明确说明：“LP87702 实现 VCC3V3 转 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50 三路电源输出”。 - 提供使能控制方式：VCC50 由 MCU 的 GPIO（MCU_5V_EN）控制，VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 由 I2C 控制。 **缺失信息**： - 电流能力（如最大输出电流）未在任何证据中给出。 - 电路文档未直接标注输出电压值，仅通过网络名推断；HSI 文档虽确认电压值，但未提供官方规格表。 #### 2. 配置信息（来自 HSI 文档） - **工作模式**（来源：证据 [4] `document:ea7b2234534382bf`） - LP87702 运行模式包括：SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE。 - 详细定义：SHUTDOWN 时 NRST 低于阈值，器件停止工作；READ OTP 时加载 OTP 配置；STANDBY 时电压转换器禁用，寄存器可读写；ACTIVE 时 DC/DC 使能。 - **使能条件与通信**（来源：证据 [6] `document:d69d04d302326d46` 和证据 [9]） - “LP87702 运行期间需要 MCU 通过 I2C 配置的寄存器”（[6]），但未列出具体寄存器地址或值。 - VCC50 使能信号：MCU_5V_EN（高电平使能输出，低电平关断）（[9]）。 - VCC1V8、VCC1V1 使能：由 MCU 通过 I2C 控制（[9]）。 - MCU 可监控中断信号（nINT）和通过 I2C 读写寄存器（[9]）。 - **寄存器配置示例（部分）**（来源：证据 [7] `document:0987620a09f7a3bf`） - 描述了 VMON_LDO1 过压/欠压反应寄存器的位定义（如 FS_I_OVUV_SAFE_REACTION1），但未给出具体项目配置值。 **缺失信息**： - 未提供具体的寄存器地址、默认值、项目所需的配置值（如输出电压选择、保护阈值等）。 - 缺少使能时序、电源排序等详细配置参数。 --- ### 缺口汇总 | 子问题 | 状态 | 缺失项 | |--------|------|--------| | sq_1：供电输出信息 | partial | 电流能力（如最大输出电流）；电路文档未直接标注输出电压值（仅靠网络名推断） | | sq_2：配置信息 | covered | 具体寄存器设置值（地址、默认值、项目配置值）未给出；使能时序、保护阈值等细节缺失 | --- **注意**：以上结论均基于给定证据，未使用任何推断或外部知识。若需更详细参数，应补充 LP87702 数据手册或完整寄存器表。

MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | None：U20; 评分上下文：0/0 段，0/0 字符；已裁剪：否
hw-v1-multihop-eth-enablesuccess0.2370.0000.286not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.636not_applicable
问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：**结论：基于现有证据，无法确定从MCU使能到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径。** 关键缺失信息如下：MCU使能信号与电源管理器件之间的连接未明确，电源管理器件的具体型号及使能机制未提供，以及1.0V电源到以太网PHY的直接连接未明确。 **支撑证据：** ### 子问题1：MCU的使能信号对应哪个引脚？该引脚连接到哪个电源管理器件或开关？ * **直接结论**：证据中未明确标识用于使能以太网PHY 1.0V电源的MCU引脚。已知的MCU使能信号（如`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5`、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`）分别连接至CAN收发器和电源管理器件U1803/U1802，但未连接至与1.0V电源相关的器件。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**：`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5` (源[10])、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` (源[12]) 和 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` (源[13]) 是MCU引脚上定义的使能信号。`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5` 连接至CAN收发器模块。`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 连接至U1803.ON，`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 连接至U1802.ON。 * **缺失信息**：未发现从MCU引脚到用于生成1.0V电源的器件（如下文子问题2中的U1501）的任何使能信号连接。 ### 子问题2：该电源管理器件如何输出1.0V？中间是否有LDO、DC-DC或滤波网络？ * **直接结论**：证据表明，存在一个名为`VCC1V0_ETH`的1.0V电源网络，该网络由器件U1501（型号未明确）输出，并包含滤波网络。但未提供该器件（U1501）的内部架构（如LDO或DC-DC）或使能引脚连接信息。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**：网络`VCC1V0_ETH` (源[15]) 连接了电容（C1420, C1506, C1507）、电感（L1403）和电阻（R1146, R1504），并连接到器件U1501的输出引脚（OUT）。这表明该网络包含滤波网络。网络`&1V0_ETH_ACQ` (源[14]) 连接至MCU的ADC引脚，可能用于电压监测。 * **缺失信息**： * 器件U1501的具体型号及内部架构（LDO或DC-DC）未提供。 * U1501的使能引脚（EN）或相关控制信号未在证据中体现。 ### 子问题3：1.0V电源最终以何种方式（走线、平面、跳线）供给以太网PHY？ * **直接结论**：证据显示以太网PHY（U1400）的电源引脚连接至网络`N22956977`，但未明确该网络是否为`VCC1V0_ETH`。未发现`VCC1V0_ETH`网络与PHY电源引脚的直接连接。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**： * PHY U1400 (源[1]) 的VDDA电源引脚连接至网络`N22956977`。 * 网络`VCC1V0_ETH` (源[15]) 连接至电容C1420，C1420位于以太网PHY模块附近。 * **缺失信息**： * 网络`N22956977`与网络`VCC1V0_ETH`之间的关系未明确，无法确认1.0V电源是否通过走线、平面或跳线供给PHY。 * 没有证据表明`VCC1V0_ETH`直接连接到U1400的任何电源引脚。
评分正文：**结论：基于现有证据，无法确定从MCU使能到以太网PHY 1.0V电源的完整路径。** 关键缺失信息如下：MCU使能信号与电源管理器件之间的连接未明确，电源管理器件的具体型号及使能机制未提供，以及1.0V电源到以太网PHY的直接连接未明确。 **支撑证据：** ### 子问题1：MCU的使能信号对应哪个引脚？该引脚连接到哪个电源管理器件或开关？ * **直接结论**：证据中未明确标识用于使能以太网PHY 1.0V电源的MCU引脚。已知的MCU使能信号（如`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5`、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`）分别连接至CAN收发器和电源管理器件U1803/U1802，但未连接至与1.0V电源相关的器件。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**：`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5` (源[10])、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` (源[12]) 和 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` (源[13]) 是MCU引脚上定义的使能信号。`CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5` 连接至CAN收发器模块。`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 连接至U1803.ON，`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 连接至U1802.ON。 * **缺失信息**：未发现从MCU引脚到用于生成1.0V电源的器件（如下文子问题2中的U1501）的任何使能信号连接。 ### 子问题2：该电源管理器件如何输出1.0V？中间是否有LDO、DC-DC或滤波网络？ * **直接结论**：证据表明，存在一个名为`VCC1V0_ETH`的1.0V电源网络，该网络由器件U1501（型号未明确）输出，并包含滤波网络。但未提供该器件（U1501）的内部架构（如LDO或DC-DC）或使能引脚连接信息。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**：网络`VCC1V0_ETH` (源[15]) 连接了电容（C1420, C1506, C1507）、电感（L1403）和电阻（R1146, R1504），并连接到器件U1501的输出引脚（OUT）。这表明该网络包含滤波网络。网络`&1V0_ETH_ACQ` (源[14]) 连接至MCU的ADC引脚，可能用于电压监测。 * **缺失信息**： * 器件U1501的具体型号及内部架构（LDO或DC-DC）未提供。 * U1501的使能引脚（EN）或相关控制信号未在证据中体现。 ### 子问题3：1.0V电源最终以何种方式（走线、平面、跳线）供给以太网PHY？ * **直接结论**：证据显示以太网PHY（U1400）的电源引脚连接至网络`N22956977`，但未明确该网络是否为`VCC1V0_ETH`。未发现`VCC1V0_ETH`网络与PHY电源引脚的直接连接。 * **支撑证据**： * **来源**：电路图`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 * **内容**： * PHY U1400 (源[1]) 的VDDA电源引脚连接至网络`N22956977`。 * 网络`VCC1V0_ETH` (源[15]) 连接至电容C1420，C1420位于以太网PHY模块附近。 * **缺失信息**： * 网络`N22956977`与网络`VCC1V0_ETH`之间的关系未明确，无法确认1.0V电源是否通过走线、平面或跳线供给PHY。 * 没有证据表明`VCC1V0_ETH`直接连接到U1400的任何电源引脚。

complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | MCU Component ：F01/ MCU;
